- **disband**: 실패하거나 취소된 작업에서 Orca 세션과 워크트리 상태를 확인하고 복구 가능한 결과를 보존한다. ([inho-team/oh-my-teams](https://github.com/inho-team/oh-my-teams/tree/HEAD/plugins/oh-my-teams/skills/disband) · ★1)
- **disband-agents**: Disband Claude Code sub-agents spawned inside the CURRENT tmux session only — kills agent panes in this session (excluding the current pane), and wipes the teams/tasks entries owned by this session. Use when the user says things like "チーム解散", "全エージェント消して", "disband all", "エージェント全部落として", "他のエージェントも消して", "クリーンな状態にして". Do NOT touch other tmux sessions. Do NOT kill the current pane. ([hikaru777/SettingOfClaudeCode](https://github.com/hikaru777/SettingOfClaudeCode/tree/HEAD/skills/disband-agents))
- **disbursement**: Move money on Orange Money B2B — pay out to a customer, collect from one, or transfer ([theYahia/WWmcp](https://github.com/theYahia/WWmcp/tree/HEAD/servers/orange-money/.claude/skills/disbursement) · ★40)
- **disbursement-execution** ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-finance-risk/scopes/credit-approval/skills/disbursement-execution) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **disbursement-payouts**: Gate de diseño de la entrega del fondo y el desembolso — la entrega como liquidación con bolsa bruta y deducciones línea a línea, validaciones previas obligatorias, cuenta bancaria verificada con periodo de enfriamiento, orden de desembolso idempotente contra el proveedor, reintentos sin doble pago, confirmación de recepción, incidencias y devoluciones bancarias, y los controles anti-fraude del punto de no retorno. Usar al modelar o tocar entrega de fondo, deducciones, cuenta bancaria del beneficiario, orden o intento de desembolso, confirmación o incidencia, y antes de cerrar cualquier cambi… ([PabloArauzCaballero/PasanakuBackend](https://github.com/PabloArauzCaballero/PasanakuBackend/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/disbursement-payouts))
- **disbursements**: Schedule B filings describe itemized disbursements. ([cabmeron/gov-bot](https://github.com/cabmeron/gov-bot/tree/HEAD/congress-agent/skills/fec/disbursements))
- **disc**: Transform a precise design (UML sequence diagrams and decision tables) into working code using the DisC methodology ([mossgreen/design-is-code-demo](https://github.com/mossgreen/design-is-code-demo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/disc) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **disc**: DEPRECATED — use /owtx and /owrx (OaW Mattermost by default). /disc still works and still means Discord — disc-server MCP tools, or crewchat's chat_* tools with backend "discord" where they exist — and still arms the doorbell. ([Wave-Engineering/claudecode-workflow](https://github.com/Wave-Engineering/claudecode-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/disc) · ★1)
- **disc**: Use when managing a Discord server through the `disc` CLI (a Go bot). Covers channels, roles, scheduled events, and server management via bots.json and server.json. Use whenever asked to create, update, delete, list, move, or inspect Discord channels/roles/events, or to snapshot and apply server state with `disc server`. ([metruzanca/disc](https://github.com/metruzanca/disc/tree/HEAD/.agents/use_disc-cli))
- **disc-assess**: DISC behavioural assessment of a person or team: identifies the style and produces a profile with communication recommendations and conflict-resolution tips. TRIGGER: "perfil DISC", "avalia o estilo comportamental", "DISC assessment", "como comunico com ele", "adapt my communication style", "/lead disc &lt;person/team&gt;". SKIP: an active dispute between two people -&gt; lead/conflict-resolve (mediation that uses DISC, not the assessment itself); whole-team dysfunction signals -&gt; lead/team-health (Lencioni model beats individual profiling). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/plugins/arkaos-leadership/skills/disc-assess) · ★18)
- **disc-assignment**: Assign or review DISC types for several people at once in an interactive table. Use when setting/correcting DISC across a group — not a single person's profile (use search for that). ([crystal-project-inc/crystal-knows-plugin](https://github.com/crystal-project-inc/crystal-knows-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/disc-assignment) · ★1)
- **disc-brake**: Disc brake design — contact pressure distribution, braking torque, thermal analysis (rotor temperature rise), fade, FMVSS 135, pad material selection, caliper design, NVH (squeal), duty cycle, ventilated rotor. ([MP-AI-20/mechanical-engineering-skills](https://github.com/MP-AI-20/mechanical-engineering-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/disc-brake) · ★13)
- **disc-conscientiousness**: วิธีเข้าใจ สื่อสาร ทำงาน และรับมือกับคนแบบ C — Conscientiousness (ละเอียด/มาตรฐาน) (DISC) — โทนการคุย ฟีดแบ็ก การโน้มน้าว และการคลายความขัดแย้ง. Trigger เมื่อพิมพ์ /disc-conscientiousness หรือถามว่า "จะคุย/รับมือ/ทำงานกับคน C — Conscientiousness (ละเอียด/มาตรฐาน) ยังไง" ([ksmaster03/claude-canfly-skill](https://github.com/ksmaster03/claude-canfly-skill/tree/HEAD/standard/disc-conscientiousness) · ★14)
- **disc-dominance**: วิธีเข้าใจ สื่อสาร ทำงาน และรับมือกับคนแบบ D — Dominance (เด่น/มุ่งผล) (DISC) — โทนการคุย ฟีดแบ็ก การโน้มน้าว และการคลายความขัดแย้ง. Trigger เมื่อพิมพ์ /disc-dominance หรือถามว่า "จะคุย/รับมือ/ทำงานกับคน D — Dominance (เด่น/มุ่งผล) ยังไง" ([ksmaster03/claude-canfly-skill](https://github.com/ksmaster03/claude-canfly-skill/tree/HEAD/standard/disc-dominance) · ★14)
- **disc-evolution-model-data-comparison**: Use when ALMA survey catalogs of 12CO line fluxes and 887-micron disc sizes need to be overlaid with disc-evolution model tracks to test viscous vs MHD-wind scenarios, or when deciding whether to select samples by flux or size criteria. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/disc-evolution-model-data-comparison))
- **disc-forge**: Burn Red Book audio CDs or MP3 data CDs on macOS via cdrdao, sourcing from a remote media host over SSH or local files. Audio CDs carry CD-Text from ID3 tags; MP3 discs use ISO9660+Joliet (~700 MB). Handles full-album burns and cherry-picked playlists. Triggers on 'burn CD', 'burn album', 'make audio CD', 'mixtape disc', 'soundtrack for the car'. ([tdimino/claude-code-minoan](https://github.com/tdimino/claude-code-minoan/tree/HEAD/skills/integration-automation/disc-forge) · ★41)
- **disc-geometry-gradient-mapping**: Use when fitting a disc transit light curve attributed to a circumsecondary disc and you need to reduce the parameter space of disc size/orientation configurations by gradient-based rejection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/disc-geometry-gradient-mapping))
- **disc-geometry-parameterisation**: Use when modelling transits of circumsecondary disc (CSD) systems with azimuthally symmetric, non-uniform optical-depth profiles, and you need theoretical light-curve gradients for every possible disc geometry before rejecting configurations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/disc-geometry-parameterisation))
- **disc-influence**: วิธีเข้าใจ สื่อสาร ทำงาน และรับมือกับคนแบบ I — Influence (จูงใจ/สังคม) (DISC) — โทนการคุย ฟีดแบ็ก การโน้มน้าว และการคลายความขัดแย้ง. Trigger เมื่อพิมพ์ /disc-influence หรือถามว่า "จะคุย/รับมือ/ทำงานกับคน I — Influence (จูงใจ/สังคม) ยังไง" ([ksmaster03/claude-canfly-skill](https://github.com/ksmaster03/claude-canfly-skill/tree/HEAD/standard/disc-influence) · ★14)
- **disc-orientation-parameter-space-rejection**: Use when modelling a transit light curve attributed to a circumsecondary disc and you need to constrain disc size and orientation by rejecting azimuthally symmetric disc geometries that cannot reproduce the observed light-curve gradient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/disc-orientation-parameter-space-rejection))
- **disc-parameter-grid-sampling**: Use when model–data agreement for 12CO fluxes (e.g. against Lupus or Upper Sco ALMA samples) is inconclusive and you need to quantify how uncertainties in initial disc mass and radius propagate into predicted flux spread. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/disc-parameter-grid-sampling))
- **disc-recovery**: Recover data from damaged CDs/DVDs, extract and organize photos from ISO images, detect corruption, and restore broken files using cross-disc twin matching. Use when working with optical disc images (ISO, VCD, DVD-Video), CD/DVD recovery, disc imaging with ddrescue, file carving from damaged media, photo extraction and organization, image corruption detection and repair, or EXIF-based photo sorting. Do NOT use for general file management, photo editing/enhancement, or non-optical-media tasks. ([tjkreddy/cd-recovery](https://github.com/tjkreddy/cd-recovery/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **disc-skill**: fixture skill for the knowledge-phase resolver self-test. ([agentic-underground/idea-to-production](https://github.com/agentic-underground/idea-to-production/tree/HEAD/scripts/context/fixtures/knowledge-phase/plugins/demo/skills/disc-skill) · ★1)
- **disc-steadiness**: วิธีเข้าใจ สื่อสาร ทำงาน และรับมือกับคนแบบ S — Steadiness (มั่นคง/ประสาน) (DISC) — โทนการคุย ฟีดแบ็ก การโน้มน้าว และการคลายความขัดแย้ง. Trigger เมื่อพิมพ์ /disc-steadiness หรือถามว่า "จะคุย/รับมือ/ทำงานกับคน S — Steadiness (มั่นคง/ประสาน) ยังไง" ([ksmaster03/claude-canfly-skill](https://github.com/ksmaster03/claude-canfly-skill/tree/HEAD/standard/disc-steadiness) · ★14)
- **disc-strengths-interpreter**: Interprets DISC assessment results into actionable behavioral profiles for individuals and teams with communication strategy, team composition analysis, and management guidance. Use when the user shares DISC scores or a profile report and says "interpret my DISC results", "what does this DISC profile mean", "analyze our team's DISC mix", or "how do I communicate with a high-D". For DISC-specific interpretation, not generic performance feedback. ([Peter-Swain-Inc/futureproof-skills](https://github.com/Peter-Swain-Inc/futureproof-skills/tree/HEAD/families/hr-people/disc-strengths-interpreter) · ★1)

Checks options against the person's living values, and notices when a value itself is up for revision. Doesn't decide — clarifies so the person can. Ends by bridging to the felt sense of the choice. - **Disclosure Is Not A Fix**: Writing down a known defect does not resolve it. Activate when about to ship something with a "known limitation", "known issue", "caveat", "disclosed", "we're aware", "for now", "not yet handled", or "advisory" note; a TODO/FIXME left in shipped code; a review that found a defect you are documenting instead of fixing; or shipping with a noted-but-unfixed problem. The honest bar - ship the cheapest mitigation, or record an explicit dated decision not to; disclosure is documentation, not resolution.
